- **tldr**: Compress a wall of text into a tight summary. Use when the user says it's too long, too much, "TL;DR", "give me the short version", or that they don't want to read all that. ([fkonovalov/codosos](https://github.com/fkonovalov/codosos/tree/HEAD/skills/tldr) · ★2)
- **tldr**: Invoke when the agent's conversational replies have gotten too long or wordy and the user wants shorter answers for the rest of the conversation. ([LandonSchropp/agent-toolkit](https://github.com/LandonSchropp/agent-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/ls-agent/skills/tldr) · ★2)
- **tldr**: Distill your previous response into its leanest form. Use when the user says "tldr", "too long", "shorter", or asks you to condense or summarise what you just said. ([lttr/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/lttr/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/writing/skills/tldr) · ★2)
- **tldr**: Summarises what just happened into a fixed section order — a TL;DR first, a middle named for what it carries, open items last — over one scope taken from the request, either the last answer, the task just finished, or the whole session. Reads the conversation, and the repo's own git state whenever the subject is work performed, so what changed is evidenced rather than remembered. Prints terminal text, writes no file, and never turns into a mode that reformats later answers. Use when the user wants a summary, recap, digest or overview of what happened, asks what changed or what is still open,… ([TitusKirch/skills](https://github.com/TitusKirch/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/work/tldr) · ★2)
- **tldr**: Compress wordy prose into a terse, scannable TL;DR. Use when asked to summarize, condense, shorten, tighten, "tl;dr", "give me the gist", "too long", or "the short version" of any text, message, or output. ([Battle-Creek-LLC/tamos](https://github.com/Battle-Creek-LLC/tamos/tree/HEAD/skills/tldr) · ★1)
- **tldr**: Compress wordy prose into a terse scannable TL;DR. Use when asked to summarize, condense, shorten, "tl;dr", "give me the short version", or tighten up any text or output. ([Battle-Creek-LLC/tamos](https://github.com/Battle-Creek-LLC/tamos/tree/HEAD/.exp/routing/conditions/both/.claude/skills/tldr) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **tldr**: Compress a wordy message or output into a terse, scannable TL;DR (tables, bullets) without losing decision-relevant content. Use when asked to summarize, condense, "tl;dr", or shorten existing prose. ([Battle-Creek-LLC/tamos](https://github.com/Battle-Creek-LLC/tamos/tree/HEAD/.exp/routing/conditions/baseline/.claude/skills/tldr) · ★1 · 2 copies)
